\caption{\textbf{Complete configuration-level uncertainty and stability audit.} Mean $h^2$ is averaged across available CV folds. ``Estimator SE'' is the median estimator-specific SE across available folds. ``Median estimator CI'' summarises the median lower and upper bounds of the fold-level estimator-specific intervals and is not a confidence interval for the cross-fold mean. Cross-fold SD/MAD/range are descriptive stability measures because the training folds overlap. NA indicates that the estimator did not return the corresponding uncertainty quantity.}
